### 2025-07-09 · Conway High School (id 9457895)

📵 Bell to Bell, No Cell! 🔕
At Conway High, we’re committed to keeping classrooms focused and distraction-free. In line with state law, students should not use personal electronic devices during the school day.

This includes:
📱 Cell phones
🎧 AirPods / Wireless headphones
⌚ Smartwatches

➡️ Devices should be turned OFF and stored in a backpack, locker, or vehicle during school hours.

🚫 Electronic devices are not to be used in:
❌ Classrooms
❌ Bathrooms
❌ Hallways
❌ Lunch areas

✅ When are devices allowed?
• Before school (must be stored before entering the building at 7:55 AM)
• After dismissal at 3:30 PM
• At CHS-approved special events

🔗 For more details, check out our FAQ here: 

https://docs.google.com/document/d/1L4sXPlEr9EEMj0J70zRaQhjvMCE8omfyyAhOuAhvZzM/edit?tab=t.0

Let’s work together to keep CHS a place where learning stays front and center! 💙🐾 #WampusCats #CHSProud #BellToBellNoCell

### 2025-07-11 · Conway High School (id 9475371)

🎉 Congratulations to Ms. Kait Smith – Arkansas Teacher of the Year Finalist! 🎉

We are thrilled to share that CHS’s very own Kait Smith has been named a finalist for the Arkansas Teacher of the Year! 🌟

Ms. Smith truly embodies what it means to be a champion for students. With 11 years of dedicated service—five in high school science and two as an ESL designee—she continues to make a profound impact, especially on our multilingual learners through courses like ESL Sheltered Biology and Environmental Science. Her passion, innovation, and unwavering commitment to building meaningful relationships are helping every student ascend. 🚀

As ADE Secretary Jacob Oliva said:

“Those selected represent some of the best of the best in the teaching profession, and I look forward to learning from them.”

We’re incredibly proud to see Ms. Smith recognized for the extraordinary work she does every day at CHS. 💙 #WampusCatPride #ArkansasTOY #CHSExcellence #TeacherOfTheYear

### 2025-07-15 · Conway High School (id 9487126)

🎉 Celebrating Excellence at Conway High! 🎉

We’re proud to announce that 37 Conway High School teachers have been awarded Merit Pay through the Arkansas Department of Education’s Merit Teacher Incentive Fund, earning a combined total of $109,000! 💰🍎

This state-funded initiative—established by the LEARNS Act—recognizes exceptional Arkansas educators who go above and beyond in their commitment to student learning. According to ADE:

"The Arkansas Merit Teacher Incentive Fund... rewards exceptional Arkansas educators who demonstrate outstanding performance and a strong commitment to student learning."

👏 Join us in celebrating the impact and dedication of our outstanding teachers. Their hard work continues to shape the future—one student at a time.

#ConwayHigh #CHSCats #ExcellenceInEducation #WeAreWampusCats

### 2025-08-06 · Conway Public Schools (id 9591422)

Bell to Bell, No Cell 🚫
As a reminder, CPSD students are expected to follow our “Bell to Bell, No Cell” expectations during the school day.📌 A student is not considered to be in possession of a personal electronic device if:It’s stored in their bag (and not accessed),It’s stored in a locker (and not accessed), orIt’s stored in their vehicle (and not accessed) — except as permitted by law.📲 Follow-up for Infractions:1️⃣ First Violation: Device Confiscation, Warning, Parent Contact, Student Pick-Up2️⃣ Second Violation: Device Confiscation, Parent Contact, Detention, Parent Pick-Up3️⃣ Third Violation: Device Confiscation, Parent Meeting, Saturday School/In-School Suspension, Parent Pick-Up🚨 Additional Violations: Device Confiscation, Parent Meeting, ISS/OSS, Parent Pick-Up🔗 View the full policy here: https://5il.co/3l9nqLet’s all work together to keep classrooms focused and distraction-free! 💙📚 #CPSD #BellToBellNoCell #StudentSuccess

### 2025-09-02 · Conway High School (id 9818406)

🚀 Big news from Conway High School!

CHS is one of the FIRST high schools  in Arkansas to partner with UALR on a pilot concurrent Cyber Security course—giving our students a complete pathway in Information Technology and Security with industry-aligned credentials in a High Demand, High Wage (H2) field. 🔐💻

We’re also one of the FIRST high schools in the state to become an authorized OpenEDG partner, which allows students to prepare for and earn an industry-recognized Python Programming credential. 🐍⚡

Both of these opportunities mean our Wampus Cats can graduate job ready and prepared for high-demand careers. 💙🤍

### 2025-09-06 · Conway Athletics (id 9865529)

The Buddy Harding Award is presented each year to an Arkansas high school coach who demonstrates excellence, leadership, and dedication to the game.

Tonight, at the UCA vs. UAPB football game, Lady Cat Head Coach Ashley Hutchcraft was honored as this year’s recipient! 💙

Named in honor of longtime coach Buddy Harding, the award is presented by the Harding family and the Sigma Tau Gamma fraternity to recognize coaches who leave a lasting impact on high school athletics.

Coach Hutchcraft also makes history as the first female coach to ever receive this prestigious honor. 🚾🏀

### 2025-09-08 · Conway High School (id 9875482)

🔥 Mrs. Bailey’s Organic Chemistry students kicked off their very first lab with a bang!
In this experiment, students created acetylene gas by reacting calcium carbide with water. They then ignited the gas, producing a flame, carbon dioxide, and water—all while seeing firsthand how hydrocarbons undergo basic types of reactions.
Hands-on science at its best! 🧪✨
#WampusCatScience #HandsOnLearning #OrganicChemistry

### 2025-09-08 · Brick Cullum (id 9877473)

Mr. Cullum's AP World History students embraced creativity to develop non-linguistic representations of a complex historical debate about the Mongols. Students had fun working in groups to discuss historical documents and analyze various perspectives. CHS Social Studies continues to strive for innovative educational opportunities!

### 2025-09-09 · Conway High School (id 9890970)

🌟 This past Saturday, CHS Spanish students joined the Hispanic Heritage Festival 2025 at Laurel Park, sponsored by Paloma Community Services! 🎉

Under the direction of Señora Tedford, our students hosted an interactive booth full of cultural fun—trivia, a photo booth, face painting, coloring pages, bookmarks, and even original toys that guests loved trying out. 🇲🇽🎨📸

We’re proud of our students for sharing their creativity and helping celebrate Hispanic heritage in such a meaningful way! 💙💛

#CHSWampusCats #HispanicHeritageMonth #CelebrateCulture

🎉 Congratulations to CHS seniors Caroline Chrysler and Will Schlientz! 🎉

Information regarding tomorrow's football games at LR Southwest. 8th grade game will start at 5:00 followed by the 9th grade game. Gates will open at 5:00. Fans will enter through the front gate. Link :  https://gofan.co/event/3852507?schoolId=AR86984 

Clear Bag Policy
All attendees must follow the LRSD clear bag policy. This policy is designed to increase safety and speed up the entry process.

One clear plastic, vinyl, or PVC bag not exceeding 12" x 6" x 12".
One clear, one-gallon plastic freezer bag (Ziploc-style).
One small clutch (no larger than 4.5" x 6.5") for personal items.
Medically necessary items will be permitted after proper inspection.
Phones, cameras, and binoculars may be carried outside of a bag or case.

### 2025-10-01 · Conway High School (id 10129636)

🌏✨ In September, Mrs. Chen’s Chinese 2 students had some very special visitors! Several Conway High School alumni—now majoring or minoring in Chinese at UCA—returned to CHS to share their passion for the language.

Students participated in an interactive grammar lesson led by the alumni, complete with prizes 🎁 and souvenirs straight from China 🇨🇳.

We’re especially proud that 4 of the 5 visiting UCA students began their Chinese journey right here at CHS with Mrs. Chen, completing 3–4 years in our program! 💙

Their visit was a great reminder that learning Chinese doesn’t stop in high school—it can open doors to exciting opportunities in college and beyond. 🚀

#WampusCats #LanguageLearning #CHSproud #FutureReady

### 2025-10-02 · Conway High School (id 10153605)

💀🎨✨ In honor of Hispanic Heritage Month, four students from Señor Escalante's Spanish class visited Ms. Milner’s Drawing II class to present on the traditional Mexican holiday Día de los Muertos! 🪦🌺 Students learned about the holiday’s history, cultural meanings, and traditions—including its connection to popular culture, like Disney's Coco 🎬🎶.

Drawing II students took visual notes and practiced the grid transfer method by drawing calaveras, or sugar skulls. They’ve now begun creating altered self-portraits featuring traditional calavera designs and colors 🎨🖌️. A selection of these final portraits will be displayed for Día de los Muertos on November 2! 

#HispanicHeritageMonth #DiaDeLosMuertos#WampusCatStudentArt #CreativityInAction

### 2025-10-06 · Conway High School (id 10181557)

📖 Mrs. Smith's Junior English students are reading and analyzing The Crucible, a 1953 play by American author Arthur Miller. ⚖️🔥

For each Act, students select a part to read—and to help them visualize the characters and the 1692 setting, they enjoy ed wearing Puritan costumes 👒🧥 befitting each character's personality and description! 🎭✨

### 2025-10-06 · Conway High School (id 10182386)

🌎✨ Last week, on October 3rd, our AP Spanish classes had the pleasure of welcoming three special guests — Mrs. Kaitlin Smith, Mrs. Lourdes Goodnight, and Mrs. Judith Mendez!

Mrs. Smith spoke about the value of being bilingual and how she wishes she could speak another language fluently. 💬 Mrs. Goodnight shared her experience growing up as a Puerto Rican student in New York, where speaking her native language was once prohibited — and how proud she is today to be bilingual. ❤️ Mrs. Mendez, a former teacher from Argentina, told students about her life in both Mexico and Argentina, her Mexican-Argentinian blended family, and the educational system in Argentina. 📚✈️

To wrap up the visit, students enjoyed a Q&A session and even got to try Argentinian candy 🍬— a sweet way to end such an inspiring cultural exchange! 💫

#APSpanish #BilingualEducation #CulturalConnections #WampusCats

Ms. Dunn’s students went on a “trip around the world” without leaving the classroom! They picked up passports 🛂, ID badges 🏷️, and tour bags 🎒 before visiting stations to explore different countries, practice English 🗣️✍️, answer questions, and get their passports stamped ✅.

### 2025-10-09 · Conway High School (id 10224647)

In Mrs. Chen’s Chinese classes, students celebrated Moon Festival 🌕 — one of the most important festivals in China and several other Asian countries. As part of the celebration, students learned about the festival’s history, traditions, and legend 📜, then enjoyed a hands-on experience making snow skin mooncakes 🥮! 
Students in Chinese 3 and AP Chinese further practiced by retelling the process of mooncake making in Chinese, using newly learned vocabulary and grammar to strengthen their language skills.
Huge thanks to Velda Lueders Realtor Coldwell Banker RPM Group - Conway AR, for her generous donation that makes the enrichment activities of the Chinese program possible throughout the year!

### 2025-10-13 · Conway High School (id 10259903)

📊😄 Statistics meets tongue twisters!

On Tuesday, Oct. 7, our Stats students put their linear regression skills to the test in a hilarious hands-on activity! Students gathered bivariate data by timing how long it took different group sizes to say tricky tongue twisters — and ended the day with the entire class joining in together.

There were lots of laughs, smiles, and data points as they learned how to analyze relationships between variables in a fun, memorable way.

Try it yourself!
🗣️ “Picky people pick Peter Pan peanut butter — it’s the only peanut butter picky people pick!”

### 2025-10-14 · Conway High School (id 10262254)

Recently, Ms. Sinsley’s Statistics students took learning around the world—literally! Using small globe beach balls, they estimated the proportion of Earth covered by water. 🌊

In groups of 4–7, students tossed the globes and recorded whether their fingers landed on water or land. Then the whole class joined in for even more trials! 🌍

Their estimates ranged from 54% to 70% water—pretty close to the true value of 71%. That’s hands-on learning at its best! 👏📈

#WampusCatScholars  #HandsOnLearning #WampusCatPride

### 2025-10-15 · Conway High School (id 10288461)

🎮 CHS is proud to announce that nine student-athletes from the Wampus Cat Esports program have been offered collegiate scholarships to continue their competitive gaming careers! 🎓💪

The offers come from Hendrix College Esports, an official NCAA Division III program competing in the Southern Collegiate Athletic Conference (SCAC), led by Coach Jordan Toland. 🏆🎯

“This is a landmark moment for our program,” said Conway High Esports Coach Brad Goodnight. “Our players have shown incredible dedication, teamwork, and resilience. To see that effort rewarded with scholarship opportunities at the collegiate level proves that Esports is opening real doors for students’ futures.” 💬✨

This marks the largest group of Conway High Esports athletes to receive scholarship offers at one time! 🙌
CHS congratulates these student-athletes on their achievements and looks forward to watching them continue their success at the next level! 💙🐾💻

📸 Students pictured: Miles Butler, Fallon Emerson, and Logan Sniegowski

🎭 CHS Drama Department Presents: 12 Angry Jurors 🎭

Following the closing arguments in a murder trial, twelve jurors must decide the fate of a young defendant. What begins as an open-and-shut case soon unravels as one juror casts doubt, forcing the others to confront their biases, tempers, and personal truths.

👏 The cast and crew of 12 Angry Jurors have been working diligently over the past two months to bring this gripping courtroom drama to life — and the wait is almost over!

📅 Saturday at 2:00 PM & 7:00 PM
📍 James H. Clark Auditorium

Available at the door — cash or check only.
$7 for students & staff | $12 for adults

### 2025-10-21 · Conway Public Schools (id 10353283)

ARKANSAS ASSET BUILDERS HOSTS FREE FAFSA HELP DAY TO GUIDE STUDENTS AND FAMILIES THROUGH FINANCIAL AID APPLICATIONArkansas Asset Builders, committed to empowering Arkansans to improve their financial well-being, is hosting a free FAFSA Help Day to assist students and their families in completing the Free Application for Federal Student Aid (FAFSA). The event will provide critical, expert guidance to ensure students access the maximum financial aid available for the 2026-2027 academic year.The FAFSA is the gateway to federal grants, loans, and work-study funds, as well as many state and institutional financial aid programs. Completing the application accurately and on time is essential for paying for college, but the process can often be confusing. This event provides professional, one-on-one assistance at absolutely no cost.FAFSA Help Day Event DetailsDate: Saturday, October 25, 2025Time: 1:00 pm – 4:00 pmLocation: Faulkner County Library (1900 Tyler Street, Conway, AR 72032)Assistance Provided: Financial aid professionals from local colleges and universities will be on-site to help students and parents navigate the FAFSA, determine contributor status, and accurately complete the application."The FAFSA is more critical than ever due to recent application changes, and filing early is key to securing available funds," said Joyia Yorgey, Executive Director of Arkansas Asset Builders. "We are dedicated to removing the financial and logistical barriers that prevent students from accessing financial resources for higher education. Our FAFSA Help Day ensures families have the expert support needed to complete this vital step toward achieving their educational and financial goals."To get the most out of the event, students and parents should attend together. However, help is still available if attending individually. Attendees should bring necessary documents for the FAFSA, including: student and parent social security numbers, studentaid.gov login credentials, and information about 2024 tax return(s), any untaxed income received in 2024, and current bank and investment account balances. Appointments are highly recommended to guarantee personalized assistance.Students and parents can sign up for an appointment and find more information about the event on the Arkansas Asset Builders website at https://arkansasassetbuilders.org/event/fafsa-help-day/ or by calling 501-503-2123.About Arkansas Asset BuildersArkansas Asset Builders desires that all residents of Arkansas have the opportunity to flourish by building their household’s financial security and stability. We believe that financial education and free tax preparation are key services that empower individuals and families to break cycles of debt and financial instability and take control of their financial future. Learn more at https://arkansasassetbuilders.org

### 2025-10-27 · Conway High School (id 10417834)

💙 Behind the Blue & White: Passion. Knowledge. Dedication.

📚 Department Spotlight: English Language Arts

If the ELA Department were a book genre, they’d be classic fiction — full of timeless themes, universal truths, and creative imagination.

✨ One word that defines them: Transformative.
Every day, CHS English teachers help students find their voice, challenge their thinking, and discover the power of words.

Through stories, essays, and conversations, they connect reading and writing to students’ lives and real-world issues — showing that literature isn’t just something you study, it’s something you live.

🖋️ What they hope every student takes away: confidence in the ability to analyze, interpret, and write with purpose.

💭 Department spirit character: Atticus Finch — thoughtful, patient, and grounded in moral integrity and the belief that education can change lives.

📖 Advice from the ELA team: Don’t be afraid to make mistakes. Every draft, every reread, every misstep brings you closer to understanding and growth.

💙 Behind the Blue & White, our ELA teachers embody passion, knowledge, and dedication — empowering every Wampus Cat to think deeply, express boldly, and grow confidently.

### 2025-10-31 · Conway High School (id 10480865)

🎃💥 Chemistry + Halloween = The ultimate science experiment!
Students in Ms. Bailey’s and Mr. Schwab’s classes learned about chemical reactions and compounds by turning pumpkins into explosive demonstrations of combustion. After reviewing lab safety and reaction theory, they mixed calcium carbide and water to produce acetylene gas — then watched as a controlled ignition blew the pumpkin’s face into place! 🔬🔥

### 2025-11-04 · Conway Public Schools (id 10508431)

📣 Help Shape the Future of Conway Public Schools! 🐾

Conway Public Schools, in partnership with the Conway Area Chamber of Commerce and Conway Development Corporation, has launched a Strategic Planning Steering Committee to help guide our district through 2026 and beyond.

We’re working with the Winthrop Rockefeller Institute (WRI) to gather input from parents, students, staff, local employers, and community members. Your feedback will help us understand what’s working well, where we can improve, and how we can best prepare Conway students for lifelong success.

Please note, the survey will close on November 30, so be sure to share your thoughts before then. This is an important opportunity for every parent to have a voice in shaping the future of Conway Public Schools. The feedback collected will inform both our Strategic Readiness Plan for 2025–2026 and the long-range strategic plan being developed by the Steering Committee.

💬 We want to hear from YOU—our community!
 Your perspective as a citizen and taxpayer is vital as we make decisions that impact our schools and our city’s future.

📩 A Parent Survey and Staff Survey has also been emailed to all CPSD families and staff, so if you have a child in or work for Conway Public Schools, please take a few minutes to complete it!

🗳️ CPSD Citizen/Taxpayer Survey:
https://forms.office.com/r/bZLfGmgjWZ 

Together, we can ensure that Conway Public Schools continues to grow, innovate, and serve our students—and our community—with excellence.
#ConwayPublicSchools #WampusCats #CPSDStrategicPlan

### 2025-11-10 · Conway High School (id 10575825)

🎉 CHS Shines at the 57th Arkansas Student Congress on Human Relations State Tournament! 🎉

Our Conway High School Student Congress team brought their A-game to this year’s state tournament, showing off their debate skills, legislative know-how, and teamwork in a competition that’s been shaping young leaders since 1968.

🏆 2025 Arkansas Student Congress Awards:

Superior in Caucus: Ian, Katharine, Amelia, Addison, Landon

Excellence in Committee: Ian, Katharine

Superior in Committee: Milina, Amelia, Addison, Landon

Excellence in Floor Debate: Milina

Superior in Floor Debate: Ian, Katharine, Addison, Landon

Superior in Parliamentary Procedure: Ian, Katharine, Addison, Landon

✨ CHS Best Delegate: Ian Lee-Kim

💡 Individual Highlights:

Highest Scoring Delegate in House I: Ian Lee-Kim

Melinda Valdez-Milloway Award: Ian Lee-Kim

House I Best Bill (2nd Place): Ian Lee-Kim (Author) & Katharine Welky (Co-author)

House I Best Delegation (1st Place): Ian Lee-Kim & Katharine Welky

House I Bill Passed in Committee: Ian & Katharine

House II Bill Passed in Committee: Milina Michel (Author) & Amelia Garrett (Co-author)

House III Elected House Clerk (2nd Year!): Addison Choate

House III Best Overall Delegate: Landon Harvey

House III Best Delegation (1st Place): Addison & Landon

House III Bill Passed in Committee: Addison (Author) & Landon (Co-author)

🏅 Overall Delegation: 2nd Place – Conway High School

The Arkansas Student Congress, the state’s oldest model legislative event, gives students the chance to experience the democratic process first-hand—writing and debating legislation, running caucuses, serving in committees, and presenting their ideas on the House & Senate floor at the State Capitol.

A huge congratulations to all our delegates for representing CHS with skill, intelligence, and teamwork! Special thanks to Mrs. Casey Griffith for coaching our talented Speech & Debate team.

### 2025-11-11 · Conway High School (id 10586539)

Congratulations to CHS Senior, Callie Wesbecher, for being named a National Speech & Debate Association’s Academic All American! 

The Academic All American award recognizes high school students who have earned the degree of Superior Distinction within the NSDA Honor Society by competing in speech and debate events at tournaments; completed at least 5 semesters of high school; demonstrated outstanding character and leadership; and earned a GPA of 3.7 or higher. Fewer than 2% of National Speech & Debate Association student members earn the Academic All American award every year. This honor is an indicator of Ian’s academic achievement and competitive success. 

Callie is a 3 year member of the CHS Speech & Debate Team. Callie competes in Congressional Debate, along with Student Congress and Mock Trial (Philadelphia). Upon graduation, Callie will be attending Central Baptist College.

### 2025-11-12 · Conway High School (id 10586554)

Congratulations to CHS Senior Ian Lee-Kim for earning Premier Distinction within the National Speech & Debate Association's Honor Society. 

Premier Distinction is the highest degree in the NSDA Honor Society! This is an outstanding achievement. Fewer than 3% of NSDA members earn Premier Distinction prior to graduation! This milestone is a celebration of the hundreds of hours Ian has spent preparing, practicing, and competing to hone his communication and leadership skills. Along with earning Premier Distinction, Ian is also an NSDA Academic All-American. 

Ian is a 3-year member of the CHS Speech & Debate team and has competed across the state of Arkansas, along with Philadelphia, Iowa, Kentucky, and Virginia. Ian competes in Congressional Debate, Mock Trial, Student Congress and Original Oratory.

### 2025-11-13 · Conway High School (id 10608398)

🧸💉 EMR Students Bring Smiles to the Teddy Bear Clinic!

Kylee Ritchey, Ronnia Evans, Mailee Cushing, Shelby Holder, Haley McCarron, and Magnolia Smyers recently took part in HIPPY Arkansas’s Teddy Bear Clinic — a fun, hands-on event that helps young children feel more comfortable with doctor and hospital visits.

Using stuffed animals as “patients,” children learned how to check vitals, give pretend shots, and apply bandages — all with help from our EMR students, who played the role of caring medical professionals.

Through this experience, students practiced real-world communication and empathy skills while supporting community health education. 💙

Way to go, EMR team, for making healthcare learning bear-y fun! 🩺🐻

### 2025-11-18 · Conway High School (id 10656715)

Our AP Biology students had a sweet time in lab on Friday—well… until the Gymnema tea kicked in! 😅🧪
As part of their Signal Transduction Pathways investigation, students sampled all the major flavors (sweet, sour, salty, bitter, and umami) before drinking Gymnema tea, which temporarily blocks the sweet receptors on your tongue. Mind. Blown. 🤯🍬

They also discovered that thanks to our unique DNA, we don’t all taste things with the same intensity! 👅🧬
Check out this snapshot from Friday’s flavorful (and slightly confusing!) experiment!

#APBiology #ScienceIsCool #CHSScience

### 2025-11-18 · Conway High School (id 10659054)

🎄 Conway High School Angel Paws Week • December 1–5 🎁

Angel Paws is a CHS tradition sponsored by Caring Cats. Each paw represents a holiday gift for a Conway High student or family in need. Students can pick up an Angel Paw in Room 258 (Mrs. Bell) all week.

To support our Angel Paw families, we’re also collecting small hygiene items during our themed dress-up days:

🌟 Monday – Christmas Morning Monday
Wear: comfy clothes
Bring: shampoo, body wash, or bars of soap

🧦 Tuesday – Sock It to Me, Santa
Wear: festive or crazy Christmas socks
Bring: hats and gloves

💙 Wednesday – Have a “Blue” Christmas
Wear: Conway blue
Bring: deodorant or face wash

🎁 Thursday – Ugly Sweater Party
Wear: your ugliest Christmas sweater
Bring: toothbrushes and toothpaste

🐾 Friday – Christmas with the Cats
Wear: Caring Cat shirt + Santa hat
Bring: Angel Paw gifts (last day to pick up a paw!)

These small items make a big difference for CHS families.
Community groups, teams, or organizations interested in sponsoring a full family may contact Mrs. Bell at Bella@conwayschools.info
.

Let’s make this season bright for our Conway High community! 💙

### 2025-11-20 · Conway High School (id 10680934)

🌎🍽️ Spanish 3 turned up the heat last week!
Señora Tedford’s classes put their cooking skills — and their Spanish — to the test. Students researched an authentic Hispanic dish, translated the recipe, cooked it at home, and then presented the full process to their classmates.

From Argentina to Chile, Spain to Colombia, Mexico to Venezuela, our students explored flavors from across the Spanish-speaking world and shared some truly delicious creations! 

What a tasty way to learn! 🌶️✨

### 2025-11-20 · Conway High School (id 10683569)

### 2025-12-01 · Conway High School (id 10747141)

Our Science Department at CHS is a powerhouse of expertise, experience, and global perspective! 🌍🔬
With 158 years of combined teaching experience and a wide array of degrees—ranging from Chemistry and Biology to Physical Science and even a Doctorate in Pharmacy—our teachers bring passion and knowledge to every classroom. The CHS Science Department offers 15 different science courses, including on-level, AP, accelerated, concurrent semester, and advanced semester courses, giving students countless opportunities to explore and excel. 🧪
Many of our science teachers have traveled the world—from Costa Rica and Italy to Canada and Puerto Rico—bringing diverse perspectives into their teaching. ✈️ Beyond the classroom, our educators shine with accomplishments like running nonprofits, creating CHS spring musical costumes, winning state fiddle championships, and launching programs at CHS like Science Helper. Their dedication has earned multiple Teacher of the Year awards and other honors, making the CHS Science Department a launching pad for future scientists and innovators. 🌿
#CHSWampusCats #CHSScienceRocks #WampusCatPride #TeachersWhoInspire

### 2025-12-01 · Conway High School (id 10746441)

Congratulations to CHS Senior, Landon Harvey, for being named a National Speech & Debate Association’s Academic All American! 

The Academic All American award recognizes high school students who have earned the degree of Superior Distinction within the NSDA Honor Society by competing in speech and debate events at tournaments; completed at least 5 semesters of high school; demonstrated outstanding character and leadership; and earned a GPA of 3.7 or higher. Fewer than 2% of National Speech & Debate Association student members earn the Academic All American award every year. This honor is an indicator of Landon’s academic achievement and competitive success. 

Landon is a 3 year member of the CHS Speech & Debate Team. Landon competes in Congressional Debate, along with being on the Student Congress team.  Upon graduation, Landon will be attending the University of Central Arkansas. Landon is coached by Mrs. Casey Griffith.

### 2025-12-05 · Conway High School (id 10799652)

🌿 Exploring Biology in the Real World! 🌄
Our Fundamentals of Biology class took learning outdoors today with a field trip to Petit Jean State Park—and what a day it was!

Students studied ecosystems and biodiversity up close, snapping photos of mosses, ferns, lichens, and mushrooms along the trail. They also got to experience some of Petit Jean’s most fascinating features: the iconic sandstone “turtle” rocks, a cave filled with ancient pictographs and petroglyphs created by Native peoples 500–1500 years ago, and even tested out some impressive echoes across the Cedar Creek canyon!

Despite the 35° temps and a surprise dusting of sleet, this crew embraced the adventure and made the most of every moment. ❄️🌲

What an unforgettable way to connect classroom learning with Arkansas’s natural treasures!

### 2025-12-08 · Conway High School (id 10822963)

Our Model UN students had an incredible showing at the Arkansas Model UN Conference on November 21–22! 🎉🌍

🏅 Rapporteurs
Jeffrey Tan, Brody Mercer, Ian Lee-Kim, and Mustfa Zia were selected by their committees as Rapporteur.

🏆 Honorable Mention Outstanding Delegates
• Human Rights Council: Emmanuel Godinez, Milna Michel, Addison Choate
• Economic & Social Council: Zenna Dean
• Economic & Financial Committee: Jeffrey Tan
• Disarmament & Security Committee: Cole Britt, Landon Wharton
• Social & Humanitarian Committee: Brody Mercer
• Council of Arab League: Sophia Scholz
• International Court of Justice: Mustfa Zia

🥇 Overall Best Delegate in Committee
• Historical Security Council: Ian Lee-Kim
• Economic & Social Council: Dylan Mercer

📄 Position Paper Awards
• Honorable Mention: Delegation of the Netherlands
• Best Position Paper: Delegations of Kenya, Libya, Mexico, and Uruguay

🏅 Delegation Awards
• The Netherlands (Landon Wharton, Brooklyn Martin, Sidney Hinojosa, Roseli Pineda, Zenna Dean, and Dylan Simmons) earned an Honorable Mention Outstanding Delegation Award, placing top 15 of 126 delegations.
• Mexico (Cole Britt, Jeffrey Tan, Brody Mercer, Dylan Mercer, Ian Lee-Kim, Milina Michel, and Mustfa Zia) won the Co-Outstanding Delegation Runner-Up Award, finishing 3rd of 126 delegations.

We’re proud of all our Conway High students for representing our school with excellence and professionalism throughout the conference! 💙🤍🌎

#StudentSuccess #GlobalCitizens #AcademicExcellence #FutureLeaders #CHSModelUN

✨ Our Spanish 4 and AP Spanish students visited the University of Central Arkansas for a campus tour completely in Spanish! 🇪🇸🎓
Our guides were bilingual UCA students who shared information about campus life, programs, and traditions. Students practiced real-world Spanish and explored UCA as a future college option. 🌟
It was raining 🌧️, but that didn’t stop us—we toured the campus under umbrellas ☂️ and made the most of our day!

### 2025-12-12 · Conway High School (id 10868293)

Our Organic Chemistry students had an exciting day at UCA! 🔬✨
They completed a steam distillation of cinnamon to isolate cinnamaldehyde, the compound that gives cinnamon its warm, familiar aroma. Afterward, Dr. Singleton helped analyze their results using IR and NMR spectroscopy to see if they successfully produced the real thing. 👩‍🔬📊

In the afternoon, ACS brought the fun with hands-on science demos, including carbon-dioxide bubble making that filled the room with smiles and suds. 🫧😄

Science days are the best days! 🌟🧪

#FutureScientists #ChemistryInAction #WampusCatsInSTEM

### 2025-12-17 · Conway High School (id 10925471)

The Conway High School Speech & Debate Team traveled to New Orleans, LA, to compete at the 47th annual Isidore Newman Speech & Debate Tournament. Competing were: Newton Nganga (novice Lincoln-Douglas debate), Ian Lee-Kim (Original Oratory), Wren Welky (Original Oratory), Kamryn Young (Informative Speaking), and Joshua Mendoza (Informative Speaking). They all did a wonderful job writing and presenting their pieces, competing against students from across the United States. 

Newton Nganga finished in 3rd place in novice Lincoln-Douglas debate. His next competition will be in January at the Saints Classic in Ridgeland, MS, where he will once again compete in Lincoln-Douglas debate. 

Wren Welky finished in 2nd place for her Original Oratory persuasive speech. She also earned her first bid to compete at the University of Kentucky Tournament of Champions in April. Her next tournament will be at the Saints Classic in Ridgeland, MS, where she will compete in Congressional Debate. 

All students are members of the CHS Speech & Debate team and are coached by Mrs. Casey Griffith.

### 2026-01-05 · Conway High School (id 11000702)

Before students headed off for Christmas break, the Conway High School National Honor Society partnered with Mrs. Lindsay Spicer’s kindergarten class at Carolyn Lewis Elementary to spread some holiday cheer 📚🎄

Mrs. Spicer hoped each of her students could head into the break with a cozy Book Buddy stuffie to cuddle up with and a Christmas book to enjoy, and NHS was excited to help make that wish a reality. Knowing the lasting impact reading can have, NHS members were proud to support this meaningful project and help foster a love of reading in our youngest learners.

Pictured with Mrs. Spicer and her kindergarten students are NHS seniors and Executive Board members Addison Choate, President; Katharine Welky, Projects Chair; and Ian Lee-Kim, Parliamentarian.

### 2026-01-12 · Conway High School (id 11054087)

The CHS Math Department is built on experience, expertise, and a love of problem solving ➗📐

With 182.5 years of combined teaching experience, our math teachers bring deep knowledge and advanced training to the classroom, including multiple master’s degrees, specialist degrees, and doctorates in education. The department offers 15 math courses, including 3 AP courses and 3 concurrent-credit courses through UACCM, giving students meaningful academic pathways and opportunities to earn college credit.

Our teachers see math as logical, empowering, and essential to problem solving, and they bring that mindset to their work every day. Recognized with honors such as Teacher of the Year awards and National Board Certification, they make the CHS Math Department a place where students build confidence and critical thinking skills.

#WampusCatPride #MathMatters #TeachersWhoInspire

🎓 Big News for Conway High Seniors—and Our Community!Joshua Mendoza, a senior at Conway High School, has matched with MIT through the QuestBridge National College Match program—earning a full ride to his dream school! Joshua also received a $250 Boost Grant from ConwayUP for completing his scholarship application.ConwayUP is a local grant program that turns community donations into $250 grants for students who complete scholarship applications. Depending on the program, those applications can unlock thousands of dollars in financial aid and life-changing opportunities.⏰ With many scholarship deadlines approaching February 1, now is the time for seniors to take action—especially while they’re on break and have time to focus.👉 Students: Learn how to qualify for a $250 Boost Grant and get on the list for scholarship support at conwayup.org💙 Community & Businesses: Every dollar donated goes directly toward $250 grants that encourage students to invest in their futures.Let’s make sure no student misses out on these opportunities.

### 2026-01-12 · Conway High School (id 11062513)

🩸 We Need You! ❤️
Conway HOSA invites students, staff, and community members to participate in our Spring Blood Drive on February 6, 2026, from 8:30 a.m. to 2:30 p.m.

As a thank-you for supporting this lifesaving cause, community members who donate will be moved to the front of the line, so you won’t have to wait long!

Every 2 seconds, someone in the United States needs blood or platelets, and nearly 29,000 units of red blood cells are needed each day. Just one donation can help save more than one life.

Join us and be the reason someone gets a second chance. Your donation truly makes a difference! ❤️🩸

📧 Questions? Contact Ms. Tillery-Harris at TilleryHarrisC@conwayschools.info

#ConwayHOSA #BloodDrive #GiveBloodSaveLives #BeTheReason #CommunityStrong #WampusCatsCare

### 2026-01-13 · Conway High School (id 11076461)

🎉 Conway High School Named to College Board AP School Honor Roll 🎉

Conway High School has earned a spot on the College Board’s AP School Honor Roll for the 2024–2025 school year, receiving Silver-level recognition!

The AP School Honor Roll recognizes schools that are expanding access to Advanced Placement courses while supporting strong student performance and college readiness.

At Conway High School, students have access to 27 AP courses, taught by 26 AP-trained teachers. Last year alone, CHS seniors took a total of 555 AP exams, with 58% earning scores of 3 or higher, qualifying for potential college credit.

This Silver distinction reflects CHS’s continued commitment to academic excellence, opportunity, and preparing students for success beyond high school. 🐾📚

#ConwayHighSchool #APHonorRoll #AcademicExcellence #CollegeReady #WampusCatPride

UCA Outreach is pleased to announce that registration is now open for their Spring ACT Prep courses.These interactive courses are offered via Zoom and are designed for high school students in 10th grade and above who have scored between 17–25 on the ACT or who have not yet taken the exam. Instruction is provided by current and former UCA faculty and staff, who will review subject material and share effective test-taking strategies to help students approach the ACT with greater confidence and efficiency.Each subject includes three instructional sessions, and students may enroll in individual subjects or choose all four at a reduced rate. To maximize learning, class sizes are limited to 12 students per section.• $40 per subject• $150 for all four subjectsThis spring, courses will be offered exclusively online via Zoom. We also welcome feedback regarding your students’ needs or topics and formats you would like to see offered in the future.Thank you for helping us share this opportunity with your students and families.Registration Link

### 2026-01-20 · Conway High School (id 11132364)

🌱 One Blade. One Minute. Infinite Stories. 🌱

Ms. Brown’s 8th period juniors took Walt Whitman’s words literally:

"I lean and loaf at my ease observing a spear of summer grass."

Each student picked a single blade of grass, stared at it for 60 seconds… then wrote for 3 minutes. The result? Giggles, wild imagination, and stories that grew taller than the grass itself! ✨

#WhitmanVibes #WampusCatsWrite #CreativeWriting

Should that occur, the following dismissal times would apply:Elementary Schools: 1:15 p.m.Secondary Schools (Middle Schools, CJHS, & CHS): 1:30 p.m.If an early release is implemented, end-of-day dismissal procedures would remain normal, buses would run their regular routes, and all after-school activities and programs would be canceled.We will communicate any decision as soon as it is made. Please continue to monitor your email, phone messages, and district communication channels for updates. ### 2026-01-23 · Conway High School (id 11174727)

The Conway High School Speech & Debate Team traveled to Ridgeland, Mississippi to compete at the Saints Classic Speech & Debate Tournament hosted at Saint Andrew’s Episcopal High School. Competing were: Bobby Batson (Congressional Debate & Extemporaneous Debate), Callie Wesbecher (Congressional Debate), Wren Welky (Congressional Debate & Original Oratory), Dylan Mercer (Congressional Debate & Extemporaneous Debate), Jessie Smith (Congressional Debate), Ian Lee-Kim (Congressional Debate), Landon Harvey (Congressional Debate), Naomi Brooks (Poetry & Prose Interpretation), Farrah Sherman (Dramatic Interpretation & Poetry Interpretation), Milina Michel (Poetry & Prose Interpretation), Addison Choate (Informative Speaking & Varsity Lincoln-Douglas Debate), Joshua Mendoza (Informative Speaking), Kamryn Young (Informative Speaking), Newton Nganga (Novice Lincoln-Douglas Debate), Annabel Champlain (Dramatic Interpretation), and Amelia Garrett (Humorous Interpretation). 

The CHS Debaters brought home a collective 2nd Place Sweepstakes Award.

Earning individual awards were: 
Congressional Debaters - Ian Lee-Kim (4th), Wren Welky (5th), Landon Harvey (6th), Jessie Smith & Callie Wesbecher (Finalists). 

Lincoln-Douglas Debate - Addison Choate (2nd Varsity) & Newton Nganga (2nd Novice).

Extemporaneous Debate - Dylan Mercer (Champion).

Dramatic Interpretation - Farrah Sherman (2nd) & Annabel Champlain (4th).

Poetry Interpretation - Farrah Sherman (2nd) & Milina Michel (Semifinalist).

Informative Speaking - Joshua Mendoza (5th) & Addison Choate (Semifinalist)

Original Oratory - Wren Welky (3rd)

Prose Interpretation - Milina Michel (Semifinalist).

Humorous Interpretation - Amelia Garrett (Semifinalist).

Earning bids to compete at the University of Kentucky Tournament of Champions in April were:
Farrah Sherman - Dramatic Interpretation & Poetry Interpretation

Ian Lee-Kim, Landon Harvey & Wren Welky - Congressional Debate

Wren Welky - Original Oratory

Earning bids to compete at the NIETOC (National Individual Events Tournament of Champions) Tournament in May were:

Farrah Sherman - Dramatic Interpretation

Wren Welky - Original Oratory

All students are members of the CHS Speech & Debate team and are coached by Mrs. Casey Griffith.

### 2026-01-30 · Conway High School (id 11267171)

📣 CHS Families — Important Update

Due to school closures from recent inclement weather, Conway High School has made several important adjustments to upcoming schedules. ATLAS Interim #2 testing has been revised and will now take place on Wednesday, February 4 only for all 10th grade students, as well as some juniors and seniors. Students will no longer test on both February 3 and 4 as originally planned.

Families should also note that students who previously made arrangements for community service or college visits on February 3 may still attend those activities. These absences will be excused with proper documentation, which should be turned in to the front office.

Additionally, 3rd 9 Weeks Interim Progress Reports will be sent home with students during WIN on Friday, February 6, and Parent–Teacher Conferences are scheduled for Wednesday, February 11 from 10:00 a.m. to 6:00 p.m.

📧 Please check your inbox for a detailed email from Dr. Kate Worley, which includes full explanations and important reminders related to these updates.

If you have any questions, families are encouraged to contact the CHS front office once campus reopens.

Thank you for your flexibility and continued support of our students.

🧬 Learning Biology, Brick by Brick! 🧱
Recently, Ms. Branscum’s Advanced Biology classes used LEGO® proteins built from specific DNA sequences to model protein synthesis—the process cells use to make the proteins essential for life. Students also explored how DNA mutations can change proteins, resulting in effects that may be harmful, neutral, or even beneficial. Science in action!#WampusCatScience #AdvancedBiology #STEMEducation

### 2026-02-03 · Conway High School (id 11302204)

Senior Parents and Students: Conway High School will be hosting a FAFSA Parent Night in conjunction with Parent/Teacher Conferences on Wednesday, February 11th from 4:30-6 pm in the CHS Lecture Hall. reasoning, problem-solving, and creativity. Identification is based on multiple measures and is not something
students need to study or prepare for.

6th.

coordinator: reynoldsa@conwayschools.info. You may also visit the website for more information.

### 2026-02-16 · Conway Public Schools (id 11444381)

Dear Conway High School Parents & Families,

We want to share information regarding conversations that may be circulating about the possibility of students choosing to leave class during the school day.

Our role as a school district is not to promote or suppress student viewpoints. Our responsibility is to ensure student safety and to maintain an orderly learning environment for all students.

When a student leaves class during the school day, it creates safety concerns and disrupts the learning environment for other students. For that reason, if a student chooses to leave class, the situation will be addressed as a behavioral and attendance matter in accordance with our student code of conduct. 

School will operate as normal. Instruction will continue throughout the day, and administrators will supervise students appropriately to ensure safety. This is not a school or district initiated activity. We encourage families to talk with their students about school expectations, safety considerations, and how choices made during the school day can impact learning for all students. 

This information has also been emailed to all Conway High School Parents and students. 

Thank you for your partnership as we continue to focus on student safety, learning, and maintaining a respectful school environment.

Conway Public Schools

New procedures will apply for LR Central game on Friday!Student AdmissionLRSD high school students attending their home school’s game must present both an LRSD Student ID and a ticket for entry.Students from other K–12 schools (inside or outside LRSD, including elementary, middle, and high school) must be accompanied by an adult (21 years or older with valid ID).No refunds will be issued for minors attempting to enter without an adult.Game Entry GuidelinesNo entry will be permitted after halftime.No refunds will be issued for late arrivals.Ticket sales will close at 4:00 PM on game day. This allows security and police staffing to be properly adjusted for crowd size and safety.Clear Bag PolicyAll attendees must follow the LRSD clear bag policy. This policy is designed to increase safety and speed up the entry process.One clear plastic, vinyl, or PVC bag not exceeding 12" x 6" x 12".One clear, one-gallon plastic freezer bag (Ziploc-style).One small clutch (no larger than 4.5" x 6.5") for personal items.Medically necessary items will be permitted after proper inspection.Phones, cameras, and binoculars may be carried outside of a bag or case.Purpose of These ProceduresThese measures are designed to ensure a safe, family-friendly, and supportive environment for all LRSD games. Increased adult supervision, controlled entry, and clear safety policies will allow students, families, and fans to enjoy the game in a respectful atmosphere.

### 2026-02-18 · Conway High School (id 11467784)

Congratulations to CHS Senior, Naomi Brooks for being named a National Speech & Debate Association’s Academic All American! 

The Academic All American award recognizes high school students who have earned the degree of Superior Distinction within the NSDA Honor Society by competing in speech and debate events at tournaments; completed at least 5 semesters of high school; demonstrated outstanding character and leadership; and earned a GPA of 3.7 or higher. Fewer than 2% of National Speech & Debate Association student members earn the Academic All American award every year. 

This honor is an indicator of Naomi’s academic achievement and competitive success. 

Naomi is a 3 year member of the CHS Speech & Debate Team. Naomi competes in Program of Oral Interpretation, Poetry & Prose Interpretation, Original Oratory, and Mock Trial.

### 2026-02-18 · Conway High School (id 11467756)

🎉🐎 2nd Annual Lunar New Year Celebration at the Faulkner County Library 🎉🐎
On Saturday, February 7th, the CJHS & CHS Asian Club, in partnership with the Faulkner County Library, local Chinese community members, and the UCA Center for Asian Languages and Cultures, proudly hosted Conway’s 2nd Lunar New Year Celebration.
With approximately 200 attendees, the event was filled with energy, learning, and fun! Families enjoyed interactive stations featuring dumpling-making, Chinese calligraphy, crafts, chopsticks games, and more. It was a wonderful opportunity for our community to come together and celebrate the Year of the Fire Horse.

A heartfelt thank-you to the Faulkner County Library for providing the space and incredible support that helped make this event possible. We are also grateful for our amazing team of 20 student volunteers from Conway High School and Conway Junior High School, along with 14 local Chinese community members, whose enthusiasm and dedication made the celebration such a success.

Special thanks to our generous supporters:
 🥟 Sakura Ocean A.Y.C.E. Sushi & Hibachi (coming soon) for the $200 donation supporting the dumpling station
 🧋 Boba Love for donating nearly 200 cups of boba tea
 🍩 Shimmery Donut for donating 50 dozen donut holes

Events like this truly highlight the cultural vibrancy of our community and the growing excitement for Chinese culture and language. Interested in learning more? Join our Chinese program or the Asian Club! 🌏❤️

#LunarNewYear #ConwayAsianClub #ChineseCulture #CommunityCelebration #YearOfTheHorse #ConwayHigh

### 2026-02-19 · Conway High School (id 11467823)

Congratulations to CHS SeniorAddison Choate for earning Premier Distinction within the National Speech & Debate Association's Honor Society. 

Premier Distinction is the highest degree in the NSDA Honor Society! This is an outstanding achievement. Fewer than 3% of NSDA members earn Premier Distinction prior to graduation! This milestone is a celebration of the hundreds of hours Addison has spent preparing, practicing, and competing to hone her communication and leadership skills. Along with earning Premier Distinction, Addison is also an NSDA Academic All-American. 

Addison is a 3-year member of the CHS Speech & Debate team and has competed across the state of Arkansas, along with Philadelphia, Iowa, Kentucky, Louisiana and Mississippi. Addison competes in Lincoln-Douglas debate, Informative Speaking, Student Congress and is a 3-year member of our Mock Trial team.

### 2026-02-23 · Conway High School (id 11516887)

🌎🎉 Congratulations to CHS senior John David Kim! 🎉🌎

John David has been named one of only 10 national winners of the Senior Travel Scholarship through the American Association of Teachers of Spanish and Portuguese and the National Spanish Exam. After earning Gold (96th percentile) and Silver (91st percentile) honors on the National Spanish Exam, he secured a 9-day study abroad experience in Salamanca, Spain, where he will study at the University of Salamanca this summer. He was also selected for the Joseph Adams Senior Scholarship through the Sociedad Honoraria Hispánica but chose to accept the national travel opportunity.

John David scored a 5 on the AP Spanish exam, will earn the Global Seal of Biliteracy this spring (B2 Working Fluency), and is an active member of Spanish Club and SHH.

We are incredibly proud of his dedication and achievement.✨ ¡Felicidades! ✨

### 2026-02-26 · Conway High School (id 11552409)

Small in Number, Mighty in Impact: Meet Our ELL Department!

They may be small in number, but our English Language Learner (ELL) team is mighty! 💪
Together, this team brings nearly 50 years of teaching experience and 3+ years each supporting ELL students. They hold bachelor’s and master’s degrees, plus ESL endorsements, and their dedication has been recognized through awards, grants, and local honors.
Beyond the classroom, they’re just as dynamic—writing rap songs to teach history, coaching Brazilian Jiu Jitsu, performing music, exploring new cultures, and celebrating students’ milestones, like being the first in their families to graduate high school in the U.S.
ELL students are resilient, and this team’s dedication helps them grow, thrive, and achieve their dreams! ❤️
#CHSLearns #ELLExcellence

### 2026-03-04 · Conway High School (id 11615626)

Huge congratulations to our incredible Six-Leg Media team! 🎥🏆

Six-Leg Media has earned the Distinguished Merit Award in the Student Television Network (STN) Broadcast Excellence Competition in the Monthly Show – News category!

STN is the largest and one of the most prestigious competitions in the country for high school media programs — and earning Distinguished Merit places Six-Leg Media among the Top 30 programs in the nation. ✨

What makes this even more impressive? For many of these students, this is their first year producing this type of broadcast content. First year. Top 30 in the country. Let that sink in.

We are beyond proud of their creativity, professionalism, and commitment to excellence. The future of media is bright — and it’s got six legs. 🐾🎬

### 2026-03-04 · Conway High School (id 11617331)

🎨✨ Student Spotlight: Pinocchio Through a New Lens ✨🎨

Our class dove deep into motifs, metaphors, and symbolism using Disney’s Pinocchio as a practice run for their Lit Circle unit. The culminating activity, “The Picture with a Thousand Words,” had students transform their analysis into interactive art pieces—full of color, craft, and creativity!

These works don’t just reflect the story—they connect to the psychology of the Hero’s Journey and our discussions throughout the year. The students knocked it out of the park, and we’re so proud to showcase their talent! 🌟

#LiteratureInAction #ClassroomCreativity #WampusCats #CHSWampusCats #SixLegPride

### 2026-03-04 · Conway High School (id 11622964)

Congratulations to the Conway High School Mock Trial team for winning the 2026 Arkansas High School Mock Trial State Tournament!!! The tournament, sponsored by the Arkansas Bar Association, was held February 27-28, 2026, in Little Rock at the Richard Sheppard Arnold United States Courthouse. Mock trial is an academic and competitive simulation of a trial-level court proceeding, where students act as attorneys and witnesses to argue a fictional criminal or civil case. Teams analyze a provided case packet, develop legal theories, and follow simplified rules of evidence to compete in courtrooms.Twenty-one teams from across Arkansas gathered to try the civil case of Justice Miller v. Jace Talent Agency. 

The team competed in four preliminary rounds against Parkview, Bentonville West, Des Arc, and then the final round against Little Rock Central High School. Conway was the only team to go undefeated, with no dropped ballots. The team also earned Star Attorney & Star Witness awards. Out of over 100 attorneys, the tournament awards the top 7 attorneys in the 4 prelim rounds. Katharine Welky and Addison Choate were named Star Attorneys. This is the 2nd year for Addison Choate to be named a Star Attorney. The tournament awards the top 5 witnesses, and Annabel Champlin and Naomi Brooks brought home 2 of the Star Witness Awards. 

The CHS team will be the team to represent Arkansas at the National High School Mock Trial Championships in May. They will travel to Des Moines, IA, to compete against the top team from each state, along with South Korea, Guam, and Northern Mariana Islands. 

All members of the CHS Mock Trial Team are members of the CHS Speech & Debate Team and are coached by Mrs. Casey Griffith. The team is also coached by volunteer attorney coach, Mr. Matt Brown.

The Conway High School Beta Club has been named a 2025–2026 recipient of the John W. Harris Leadership Award — one of the highest recognitions in National Beta.

Named after Beta’s founder, John W. Harris, this award celebrates clubs that truly live out Beta’s four pillars: achievement, character, leadership, and service. ### 2026-03-06 · Conway High School (id 11648833)

Ms. Dunn’s class wrapped up Black History Month with an inspiring project that combined research, creativity, and real-world problem solving! 🖤

Students first researched influential African Americans and explored the 5 W’s of their impact. They also learned about African American inventors whose ideas still shape the world today.

Inspired by these innovators, students then created their own solutions to real-world problems. Ideas included an AI hairbrush to recommend hair care products, translation glasses to help people communicate across languages, and a hotel-style housing program that would provide shelter, job training, and counseling for people experiencing homelessness.

Students also tackled issues like climate change, world hunger, water quality, school safety, traffic solutions, and even disease detection.

We’re proud of the creativity, innovation, and thoughtful problem-solving from Ms. Dunn’s class! 💡

#WampusCats #BlackHistoryMonth #StudentInnovation

### 2026-03-09 · Conway High School (id 11668303)

🌎🍽️ Spanish IV students explored culture, cuisine, and connection!

Sabores de la Cultura | Flavors of Culture: Students learned vocabulary around food and discovered how dishes like Rosca de Reyes and panettone play an important role in cultural celebrations.

Recetas Auténticas | Authentic Recipes: From researching traditional Hispanic recipes to measuring ingredients and cooking, students prepared arepas, baked bread, and horchata, practicing language skills while experiencing authentic cultural traditions.

Conectando Culturas | Connecting Cultures: The unit culminated in a Zoom exchange with students from Montemayor School in Zipaquira, Colombia, where our students practiced Spanish and their peers practiced English, sharing perspectives on nutrition, celebrations, and the role of food in daily life.

A meaningful journey connecting language, culture, and real-world experiences! 🌟

#CHSWampusCats #CHSLanguageLearners #SaboresDeLaCultura #SpanishIV #CHSCultureConnection

🎉 Huge Congratulations to Mrs. Chen’s Chinese III Students! 🎉

We are thrilled to celebrate the outstanding achievements of all 13 Chinese III students who passed the HSK Chinese Proficiency Test with exceptional results! The HSK (Hànyǔ Shuǐpíng Kǎoshì) is an internationally recognized standardized test of Chinese language proficiency, and this entire group demonstrated remarkable dedication, earning a 100% passing rate.

✨ Special Highlights:
 • Four students scored an impressive 90% or above.
 • A huge shout-out to Madison Johnson, our amazing junior, who achieved a perfect 100% score! As a non-heritage speaker, this accomplishment is truly extraordinary. 🌟

These students are now on track to apply for the Arkansas Seal of Biliteracy this spring, reflecting their hard work, perseverance, and growing global competence.

To celebrate this milestone, Mrs. Chen hosted a joyful celebration featuring boba tea, donuts, and Pocky. Special thanks to Boba Love and Shimmery Donuts for generously donating delicious treats. 🧋🍩

We are incredibly proud of our students and our Chinese program. Hard work and perseverance truly pay off! 👏🎊

🌍 Expanding Horizons: Meet Our World Languages Department!

With nearly 90 years of combined teaching experience, our Foreign Language Department opens doors to the world every single day.

From Spanish, French, and Mandarin to Russian and regional Chinese dialects, this team brings true global perspective to the classroom. Several teachers have international roots — including Colombia and China — and many have lived, studied, or worked abroad. From careers in international logistics to building Chinese language programs and organizing Lunar New Year celebrations in Faulkner County, their real-world experiences enrich every lesson.

They hold multiple bachelor’s and master’s degrees, advanced certifications, international diplomas, and even National Board Certification. 🏅

When asked to describe learning a new language, they chose words like:
Rewarding. Humbling. Revelatory. A superpower.

Because language isn’t just vocabulary and grammar — it’s connection, culture, opportunity, and understanding.

### 2026-03-11 · Conway High School (id 11677746)

Congratulations to CHS Junior, Joshua Mendoza for being named a National Speech & Debate Association’s Academic All American! 

The Academic All American award recognizes high school students who have earned the degree of Superior Distinction within the NSDA Honor Society by competing in speech and debate events at tournaments; completed at least 5 semesters of high school; demonstrated outstanding character and leadership; and earned a GPA of 3.7 or higher. Fewer than 2% of National Speech & Debate Association student members earn the Academic All American award every year. This honor is an indicator of Joshua’s academic achievement and competitive success. 

Joshua is a 3-year member of the CHS Speech & Debate team and has competed across the state of Arkansas, along with tournaments in Virginia, and Mississippi. Joshua competes in Big Question Debate, Informative Speaking, and Congressional Debate.

### 2026-03-16 · Conway High School (id 11732708)

Meet the Minds Behind Conway’s Computer Science! 💻

Our Computer Science teachers bring over 70 years of combined experience, advanced degrees, and accolades—including Arkansas CS Teacher of the Year and National Board Certification.

Students can explore six CS courses, from Intro to Computer Science to AP CSA, AP CSP, Game Design II, Independent Study, and a pilot AP Cybersecurity class. Beyond the classroom, they can join Robotics (3 teams, 50+ members, 2 teams qualified for state) or Esports, competing in games like Mario Kart, Smash Brothers, Valorant, and more.

Conway CS students are state champions and national competitors, earning multiple All-State Coding Competition wins, High School Programming Competition awards, and top placements in HighSchoolHack and CTF events.

Outside of school, teachers enjoy everything from farming and running a whatnot store to hunting, traveling, and coaching sports.

#WampusCats #ConwayHigh #ComputerScience

### 2026-03-19 · Conway High School (id 11765163)

Ms. Bowden’s AP Biology students took learning beyond the classroom with an unforgettable, hands-on field trip experience! 🧬🔬

The group visited the National Center for Toxicological Research and the FDA Arkansas Human and Animal Food Laboratory, where students saw firsthand how cutting-edge biology, chemistry, and physics are used in biomedical research and to protect the safety of food for both people and pets. They even stepped into the role of scientists—participating in sensory testing of seafood and learning how experts detect insect and rodent contamination in consumer products!

After lunch, the learning continued at the Witt Stephens Jr. Central Arkansas Nature Center in Little Rock. Students explored Arkansas’s wetlands, observed live and preserved animals, and gained a deeper appreciation for conservation efforts across the state.

Experiences like this bring science to life and show our students the real-world impact of what they’re learning every day! 🌎✨

#APBiology #CHSWampusCats #STEMEducation #HandsOnLearning #ScienceInAction

The 2026 CHS Prom, "Take Me to Paradise" is Saturday April 18th from 8-11pm in the CHS Cafeteria.  This dance is for CHS 11th & 12th graders & and their dates. Current 10th grade students may only attend Prom as the date of an upperclassman. No 9th grade or below students or anyone over the age of 20 will be allowed entry.Tickets will be sold for $30 each starting Monday, April 13th-April 17th during both lunches in the cafeteria. (Cash or check only) Online tickets will be sold via GoFan Friday, April 10th-April 17th. All sales end at 1pm Friday, April 17th.NO TICKETS WILL BE SOLD AT THE DOOR & NO REFUNDS WILL BE GIVEN.Students bringing a non-CHS student as a date must pick up an Out of School Guest Form in the main office. These forms must be filled out by the guest student’s parent/guardian & principal. Out of school date forms are due back to the front office by 3:30pm Wednesday, April 15. All students attending the dance must have a ticket and photo ID.

This morning at approximately 9:43 a.m., Conway High School was placed in a brief HOLD while a student received medical attention. During a HOLD, students remain in their classrooms and teachers continue with instruction, disregarding bells until directed otherwise.

Thank you for your understanding.

### 2026-04-13 · Jamie Bratton (id 11973023)

The Wampus Cat yearbook program has earned the Jostens 2026 National Yearbook Program of Excellence award at the Silver Level. This year marks the 80th edition of the school's yearbook and the 10th consecutive year that the program has received this national honor. Under the direction of adviser Jamie Bratton and editors Anna Liles, Sylvia King, and Lauren Simpson, the staff is nationally recognized as a program that creates an inclusive book which reflects the entire student body. The program stands out as one of the best in the country because of its efficient management and excellent communication during the production process. By meeting high standards for school engagement and organization, the staff repeatedly proves they can successfully handle the challenges of professional journalism and design. This award highlights the team's dedication to telling the school’s story through a high-quality, well-managed publication.

### 2026-04-13 · Conway High School (id 11975384)

🔬🎓 Exploring the Future of Science & Healthcare! 🎓🔬

Ms. Bowden’s UACCM Concurrent Microbiology students recently had an incredible opportunity to visit University of Central Arkansas for a hands-on, behind-the-scenes look at college-level science and healthcare programs.

During their visit, students met with Dr. Kari Naylor, microbiologist, who led a tour of the life sciences labs and student spaces while sharing insights into college life and how to get involved in undergraduate research. Students also participated in a two-hour lab experience where they practiced key microbiology skills—identifying bacterial cell morphology and endospores using staining techniques and microscopy with samples they had previously collected.

The day continued with a visit to the nursing simulation lab, where Dr. Susan Gatto introduced students to "high-fidelity manikins" and discussed pathways into the nursing program and career opportunities in the field. Students also connected with Dr. Chris Ryan from Occupational Therapy, along with guest speakers from Physical Therapy and Speech Pathology, who shared valuable information about their programs, acceptance criteria, and career paths.

This immersive experience gave students a meaningful glimpse into future possibilities in science, healthcare, and beyond. Thank you to everyone at UCA who made this day so impactful!

#CHS #WampusCats #FutureReady #STEM #HealthcareCareers

### 2026-04-13 · Conway High School (id 11975678)

Congratulations to CHS Seniors Ian Lee-Kim and Addison Choate for qualifying to represent Arkansas at the National Speech & Debate’s NATIONAL TOURNAMENT this June in Richmond, Virginia. 

Ian will compete in Congressional Debate- House. Only 6 students were selected to represent Arkansas in Congressional Debate- House.  Ian is a 2-year NSDA National Tournament Qualifier. Ian was selected to represent Arkansas in Original Oratory at the 2025 tournament.  

Addison will compete in Informative Speaking. Only 2 students were selected to represent Arkansas in Informative Speaking. Addison traveled to the NSDA National Tournament in 2025 and competed in the supplemental event of Expository Speaking. 

Katharine Welky was selected as 1st Alternate in Congressional Debate- Senate. 
Annabel Champlin was selected as 2nd Alternate in Dramatic Interpretation. 

Our entire Congressional Debate delegation was awarded the District Sweepstakes Award in Congressional Debate! This is the first time in our team history to have the highest scoring delegation. Members of our delegation were: Katharine Welky and Landon Harvey in the Senate. Ian Lee-Kim, Roseli Pineda, Amelia Garrett, Milina Michel, Hugh Lamey, and Dylan Simmons in the House. 

The CHS Speech & Debate Team is coached by Mrs. Casey Griffith.

### 2026-04-15 · Conway Public Schools (id 11997377)

CPSD Staff & Families, 

We want to provide another follow-up regarding the image circulating on social media last night that contained a threatening message.

The Conway Police Department shared:
“At approximately 10:00 p.m., detectives with the Conway Police Department took a 13-year-old female Conway Student into custody in connection with reported threats made on social media.
The juvenile has been charged with Threatening to Commit an Act of Mass Violence on School Property, a Class C felony, and is currently being held at a juvenile detention center.
At this time, no other suspects are believed to be involved.
This incident remains under active investigation.
We appreciate the public’s cooperation and assistance throughout this investigation.”


School will be in session today. The safety and security of our students and staff remain our top priority.

We want to extend our sincere appreciation to the Conway Police Department and our School Resource Officers for their swift response and dedication in handling this situation. Their ability to act quickly and work thoroughly was instrumental in helping ensure the safety of our students and staff.

### 2026-04-15 · Conway High School (id 12005829)

🎶 Conway High School Band Earns Top Honors at State Contest! 🎶

The Conway High School Band’s Wind Symphony and Symphonic Band delivered outstanding performances at the Arkansas School Band and Orchestra Association’s State Concert Contest on Tuesday, April 14th in Cabot.

After winning the DD-7A (2nd bands) classification last year, the Symphonic Band returned as the Honor Ensemble this year—earning a 1st Division Rating. As the Honor Ensemble, they were not eligible for ranked competition, but their performance once again showcased their excellence.

The Wind Symphony performed in Class 7A (1st bands), earning a 1st Division Rating and being named State Runner-Up “Reserve Honor Ensemble” for the second consecutive year—an incredible achievement!

The Conway High School Band continues to stand out as one of the most decorated programs in the state, known for its superior musicianship and artistry.

📍 The attached photos highlight their recent performance at the Windgate Center for Fine and Performing Arts on the UCA campus.

👏 Congratulations to all of these talented students on their continued success! #WampusCatPride #CHSBand #ExcellenceInMusic

Students, As the week comes to a close, Conway Public Schools wants to speak directly to you. This week brought challenges that required attention from school teachers, staff, SROs, the Conway Police Department, and the Conway Fire Department. Schools remained safe because teachers and staff stepped up, stayed focused, and handled situations the right way, while you, our students, continued to do the right things.Now it’s time to move forward.The expectations are simple and important for maintaining a safe and productive learning environment. Each day, come to school ready to learn, support one another, and make good decisions.Please remember that your actions have consequences. Choices, especially those involving threats or inappropriate behavior on social media, are taken seriously and will be addressed accordingly.More importantly, Conway Public Schools believes in its students. There is confidence in your ability to lead, look out for one another, and represent your school and community the right way.It’s time to get back to work and finish the week strong.Have a great Friday and Go Wampus Cats.Conway Administration

### 2026-04-17 · Conway High School (id 12032112)

🎉 Big News from Conway at AYAA! 🎉

For the first time since 2019, Conway returned to the Arkansas Young Artists Association (AYAA) Competition—and made a powerful impact!

This statewide event brings together 60+ schools and over 1,300 student artists from across Arkansas. Our students rose to the occasion with incredible success:

🏆 Over 70 CHS and CJHS students participated
🏆 14 total awards earned
🏆 12 awards from CHS students
🏆 2 awards from CJHS students

We are extremely proud of the creativity, dedication, and talent our students showcased throughout the competition. This marks an exciting return for Conway, and we are already looking forward to building on this momentum and bringing home even more recognition in the years ahead!

Congratulations to all of our student artists! 🎨✨

### 2026-04-17 · Conway High School (id 12035897)

Good afternoon parents and guardians,CHS will be administering the ATLAS Assessments on April 20th and 21st, as well as April 28th and 29th. Testing will begin at 8am and end around 10:30am. Please make sure your students are present and refrain from checking them out until the testing period is over.As ATLAS testing approaches, we wanted to share a few important details and ways you can help support your students in doing their very best. These assessments are an opportunity for students to show what they have learned and the growth they have made throughout the year.To help encourage strong effort, we will once again offer incentives for student growth and achievement. ATLAS results may also play a role in your student’s schedule for the upcoming school year. Students who score in the bottom 25% may be placed in an Academic Intervention period. This would be a supportive class designed to provide extra help and ensure students have the skills they need to be successful.You can support your student by encouraging them to take their time, stay focused, and give their best effort on each test. Making sure they get a good night’s sleep and arrive at school on time will also help them feel prepared and confident. Please also be sure to remind them to come to school with a fully charged chromebook and charger.Thank you for your continued support. If you have any questions, please feel free to reach out.

### 2026-04-17 · Conway High School (id 12035939)

Good afternoon students,As ATLAS testing approaches, we want to remind you that this is your opportunity to show what you’ve learned and the progress you’ve made this year! Your effort on these assessments truly matters, and we encourage you to take them seriously and give your best.Just like in the past, there is an incentive for showing growth! Students who demonstrate growth from summative to summative may earn an additional day toward their semester test exemptions. In addition, we want to help incentivize achievement, so for every Level 3 or 4 a student achieves, they will earn an entry for various raffle prizes. This would be a great time for you to log into your ATLAS portal and review your ATLAS scores from last year’s summative. Many of you are already on track to beat that score based on your interim results! Now is the time to really give it your best!It’s also important to know that your ATLAS scores may impact your schedule for next school year. Students who score in the bottom 25% may be placed in an Academic Intervention period. This is a support designed to help you strengthen skills and be successful moving forward.You’re capable of doing well on this assessment. Take your time, stay focused, and put in your best effort on every question. Make sure you get a good night’s sleep and come prepared on testing days with your chromebook fully charged. It’s also a good idea to bring your charger.We’re rooting for you and excited to see what you can do!

### 2026-04-18 · Conway High School (id 12035870)

Good morning teachers,As ATLAS testing approaches, we wanted to share a few reminders and opportunities to help support and encourage students to put forth their very best effort. While these assessments do play a role in our school letter grade and teacher VAM scores, they are also an important opportunity for students to demonstrate their growth and progress.As in the past, we will continue to incentivize student growth by offering an additional day toward semester test exemptions for students who grow their Average Scale Score by 1 point or more from last year’s summative. In addition, we want to help incentivize achievement, so for every Level 3 or 4 a student achieves, they will earn an entry for various raffle prizes.  Please take a moment to highlight this opportunity with your students and ensure that they understand we are tracking growth now from summative to summative. It is also important to communicate to students that their ATLAS performance may impact their course schedule for the upcoming school year. Students who score in the bottom 25% may be placed in an Academic Intervention period. This is designed as a support system to provide targeted help and ensure continued growth.Thank you for the work you do each day to support and motivate our students. Your encouragement plays a critical role in helping them take ownership of their learning and perform at their highest level. Please let us know if you need additional materials or support as we prepare for testing.We appreciate all you do!
