### 2026-04-20 · Conway High School (id 12035877)

Good morning teachers,As ATLAS testing approaches, we wanted to share a few reminders and opportunities to help support and encourage students to put forth their very best effort. While these assessments do play a role in our school letter grade and teacher VAM scores, they are also an important opportunity for students to demonstrate their growth and progress.As in the past, we will continue to incentivize student growth by offering an additional day toward semester test exemptions for students who grow their Average Scale Score by 1 point or more from last year’s summative. In addition, we want to help incentivize achievement, so for every Level 3 or 4 a student achieves, they will earn an entry for various raffle prizes.  Please take a moment to highlight this opportunity with your students and ensure that they understand we are tracking growth now from summative to summative. It is also important to communicate to students that their ATLAS performance may impact their course schedule for the upcoming school year. Students who score in the bottom 25% may be placed in an Academic Intervention period. This is designed as a support system to provide targeted help and ensure continued growth.Thank you for the work you do each day to support and motivate our students. Your encouragement plays a critical role in helping them take ownership of their learning and perform at their highest level. Please let us know if you need additional materials or support as we prepare for testing.We appreciate all you do!

### 2026-05-01 · Conway High School (id 12189366)

Last week, students from Mrs. Chen's Chinese classes visited the University of Central Arkansas to celebrate UN Chinese Language Day! 🚌 They had the opportunity to connect with Chinese professors and CHS alumni who are now majoring in Chinese at UCA. Highlights of the visit included playing fun Chinese games with college students, exploring the Arkansas Chinese Heritage Project, and enjoying a delicious Chinese lunch provided by the UCA Center for Asian Languages and Cultures. It was a wonderful day of language learning, cultural exchange, and inspiration for students exploring Chinese language and culture!✨🏮

More appreciation fun is still to come! 💫 #TeacherAppreciationWeek #CHS #WampusCats

### 2026-05-18 · Conway Athletics (id 12371733)

Conway Baseball 9th -12th Grade Tryouts

Tryout dates for Conway High School and Conway Junior High School students wanting to participate in baseball for the 2025-2026 school year will be May 26-28 at the Conway Baseball Field from 3:00-5:00. A current physical must be entered into DragonFly Max in order to participate. . Email hardinl@conwayschools.info to sign up.



GET STARTED 
WITH DRAGONFLY

PARENTS & STUDENTS

1 Download the DragonFly MAX app from the App Store or Google Play.

2 Tap ‘Get Started’ and ‘Sign Up for Free’ then follow the prompts to create your
Parent account with your own email address.
Note: please do not create an account with your child’s name or contact information — you will get 
the chance to add your child soon!

3. Verify your account with the verification ID sent to your email address.

4 Tap ‘Connect to your school’ to select ‘Parent’ as your role and search for your
child’s school.

5. After selecting your child’s school, tap ‘Join’ to request access. An administrator
at your school will approve your request.

6 Tap ‘Set up your children’ and follow the prompts to add your kid(s) and fill out forms

### 2026-05-22 · Kameron Wright (id 12420815)

With the conclusion of the decathlon competition in Springdale, AR, these two Wampus Cats battled through ten grueling events over the course of two days. The decathlon is one of the toughest challenges in track and field, featuring the 100‑meter dash, long jump, shot put, high jump, 400‑meter dash, 110‑meter hurdles, discus throw, pole vault, triple jump, and the 1500‑meter run.

Out of 66 competitors, Gavin Garrison finished 12th overall and Cardagan Hicks finished 36th overall. Huge congratulations to these athletes for showing the mental and physical toughness required to compete in such a demanding event.

### 2026-05-26 · Conway High School (id 12444580)

❤️ Conway High School is honored to be recognized as a Heart Safe School through the Arkansas Children’s Project ADAM program!

This designation means CHS has taken important steps to prepare for cardiac emergencies through CPR/AED training, emergency response planning, and campus-wide preparedness measures designed to protect students, staff, and visitors. Project ADAM’s mission is to help schools respond quickly and confidently when every second matters.

We are incredibly thankful for the dedication of our nurses, staff, administrators, and community partners who helped make this possible. Their commitment helps ensure Conway High School remains a safer place for everyone who walks through our doors. ❤️🐾

#ConwayHighSchool #WampusCats #HeartSafeSchool #ProjectADAM #CatStrong

### 2026-06-01 · Conway High School (id 12447345)

📢 Important Immunization Reminder for Conway High School Students & Families

Students age 16 or older are required to have a second dose of the meningococcal vaccine (MCV4) if their first dose was received before age 16. This is a state-mandated requirement for school attendance.

Appointments may be made with:
✔️ Your healthcare provider
✔️ Faulkner County Health Department — 501-450-4941
✔️ Most local pharmacies

Please submit updated immunization records to Conway High School:
📠 Fax: 501-513-0031
Attention: School Nurse

📧 Records may also be emailed to Amy Kordsmeier, RN at:
kordsmeiera@conwayschools.info

Immunization exemptions may be obtained through the Arkansas Department of Health Immunization Division:
https://onlineimmunizationexemption.adh.arkansas.gov

Thank you for helping us keep our students healthy and compliant for the upcoming school year! 💙

### 2026-06-09 · Conway High School (id 12524162)

☀️ We hope everyone is enjoying a wonderful summer! As you make plans for the months ahead, be sure to mark your calendars for Conway High School Schedule Pickup.

📅 Schedule Pickup Dates

🎓 Seniors: Monday, August 3

🎓 Juniors: Tuesday, August 4

🎓 Sophomores: Wednesday, August 5

⏰ Pickup Hours
8:00–11:30 a.m. and 12:30–4:00 p.m.

Please note that these times are open to all students within the assigned grade level.

During schedule pickup, students may:
✔️ Meet with their counselor (if needed)
✔️ Receive their class schedule
✔️ Have their school ID made (if needed)
✔️ Reserve a locker (if needed)
✔️ Purchase a parking pass (if needed)

🪪 All students are required to have a school-issued ID. IDs are used for access to school buildings and campus services, and students will keep the same ID through graduation.

📣 Additional information and reminders will be shared as we get closer to schedule pickup. Enjoy the rest of your summer, and we'll see you in August!

### 2026-06-26 · Conway High School (id 12587857)

📚✨ This summer, Conway High School was proud to host Arkansas AIMS for a week of incredible professional development focused on strengthening teaching and learning across Arkansas.

More than 300 math, science, and English educators from districts across the state — alongside our own Conway educators — joined us for content-specific learning designed to deepen content knowledge, align instruction to Arkansas standards, and help prepare students for ATLAS testing.

We appreciate the opportunity to continue strengthening our partnership with Arkansas AIMS and value the important work being done to support educators through meaningful, content-specific professional learning. 🤝💙

#ConwayHighSchool #ConwayPublicSchools #ArkansasAIMS #ProfessionalLearning

### 2026-06-26 · Conway High School (id 12588881)

CHS is proud to announce Gabriella Ponce as the recipient of the Cole Jett Memorial Scholarship.

Established by Cole’s family, this scholarship honors Cole’s legacy of kindness, strength, and compassion while providing students with opportunities to further their education and pursue their dreams.

Gabriella exemplifies the values this scholarship represents, and we are honored to support her educational journey. We know she will continue to make a positive impact in her community and inspire others along the way.

Please join us in congratulating Gabriella on this well-deserved achievement. 

💙 In memory of 2026 Conway High School graduate, Cole Jett. 

May we continue to live by the words that define his legacy:

Be Kind.
Be Strong.
Be the Light.
